Conclusion: 这些发现为理解IGR J17091-3624在2022年爆发初始阶段内吸积流几何结构的演化提供了重要见解。

Abstract: We present the evolution of rapid X-ray timing variability in the black hole X-ray binary IGR J17091-3624 during the initial phase of its 2022 outburst using a ~3-day-long AstroSat observation. Utilizing the high time-resolution capability of the Large Area X-ray Proportional Counter, we perform a comprehensive study of the evolution of Type-C quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) throughout the observation. The QPO centroid frequency evolves from 3.30 to 7.78 Hz, accompanied by the broad-band noise characteristics. We further investigate the energy dependence of the fractional rms variability and time lags, finding that the fractional rms exhibits a positive energy dependence, with its amplitude evolving throughout the observation, while the time lags exhibit the soft-lag behavior and evolve with QPO frequency. These findings provide important insights into the evolution of geometry of the inner accretion flow during the onset phase of the 2022 outburst of IGR J17091-3624.

</details>


### [103] [Multiwavelength study of non-thermal emission in the Swift J1834.9-0846/W41 region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14356)
*Manoel F. Sousa,Rita C. Dos Anjos,Vitor de Souza*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过MCMC采样对Swift J1834-0846/W41区域的宽带能谱进行建模，发现纯轻子模型因磁场过低而被排除，轻子-强子模型支持超新星遗迹有效加速宇宙线；双成分场景中，延伸TeV辐射来自W41的强子作用，中心成分来自磁星风云，暗示初始自转周期P0≲0.2秒；CTA模拟显示30小时观测可区分两种形态并扩展能谱至~10 TeV以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Swift J1834-0846/W41区域非热辐射的起源，特别是HESS J1834-087的双成分TeV结构，区分不同形态配置和辐射机制。

Method: 利用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛采样在轻子和轻子-强子框架下对射电至TeV的宽带能谱进行建模，并模拟切伦科夫望远镜阵列（CTA）的观测能力。

Result: 纯轻子模型因磁场强度过低被排除；轻子-强子模型可行且与超新星遗迹加速宇宙线一致。双成分场景中，延伸辐射来自W41的强子过程，中心成分来自磁星风云，P0≲0.2秒。CTA 30小时可区分形态并测量>10 TeV能谱。

Conclusion: Swift J1834-0846/W41区域存在双成分辐射机制：延伸TeV辐射源于超新星遗迹W41的强子过程，中心TeV辐射源于Swift J1834-0846磁星风云的轻子过程。CTA未来观测可有效检验该模型。

Abstract: We investigate the origin of non-thermal emission from the Swift J1834-0846/W41 region by modeling its broadband spectral energy distribution from radio to TeV energies within leptonic and lepto-hadronic frameworks using Markov Chain Monte Carlo sampling. Motivated by morphological studies of HESS J1834-087 suggesting a two-component TeV structure, we explore a single extended source scenario and a configuration comprising a central point-like component embedded within extended emission. Purely leptonic models are disfavored in both scenarios by unrealistically low magnetic field strengths, whereas lepto-hadronic solutions yield field intensities and non-thermal energy budgets consistent with an evolved supernova remnant undergoing efficient cosmic-ray acceleration. In the two-component scenario, hadronic interactions dominate the extended TeV emission from W41, while the central excess is well described by a leptonic magnetar wind nebula powered by Swift J1834-0846, implying a short initial spin period of $P_0 \lesssim 0.2$ s. Simulated observations with the Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory show that 30 h exposures will discriminate between the two morphological configurations and extend spectral measurements beyond $\sim$10 TeV.

</details>


### [104] [CRESCENDO II: Spectral cosmic rays with improved energy losses and realistic supernova seeding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14363)
*Daniel Karner,Ludwig M. Böss,Klaus Dolag,Ildar Khabibullin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文改进了OpenGadget3中谱分辨宇宙线求解器CRESCENDO，实现了质子与电子的多种能量损失过程、改进能量压力计算（超越极端相对论近似）、超新星遗迹CR注入子网格模型，并测试了这些新过程及数值细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙线对星系和星系团中的重子反馈及非热辐射有重要影响，但几乎所有输运、加速和损失过程都依赖能量，因此需要对CR种群进行谱分辨处理以提升数值描述的准确性。

Method: 在OpenGadget3的谱CR求解器CRESCENDO中，实现了质子与电子的多种新能量损失过程，改进了超越极端相对论近似的能量压力计算，并提出了超新星遗迹CR注入的子网格模型，在有恒星形成的区域注入物理解释的谱。

Result: 在理想化设置中测试了新损失过程和CR注入与恒星形成的耦合；展示了强子损失建模为连续或灾变过程的数值差异、灵活谱截断的优势以及放弃极端相对论近似的益处；发现使用解析近似计算能量通量会导致斜率重构失败。

Conclusion: 该谱分辨宇宙线模型未来应用于大规模全物理宇宙学模拟，将有助于建立CR成分在星系演化中微观物理与宏观物理之间稳健且可观测验证的联系。

Abstract: Context. Cosmological simulation codes with subgrid models for cosmic rays (CRs) help us better understand their impact on baryonic feedback and non-thermal radiation in galaxies and galaxy clusters. An accurate numerical description requires a spectrally resolved treatment of the CR population, because virtually all transport, acceleration and loss processes depend on energy. Aims. We advance the treatment of CR electrons and protons in the on-the-fly spectral CR solver CRESCENDO in OpenGadget3. Methods. We implement several new energy loss processes for both protons and electrons and improve the computation of their energies and pressures beyond the ultra-relativistic approximation. Moreover, we present a subgrid model for CR seeding by supernova remnants, in which physically motivated spectra are injected at sites of ongoing star formation. Results. We test the newly implemented loss processes and the coupling between CR injection and star formation in idealized setups. We also highlight numerical subtleties, such as the differences arising when hadronic losses are modelled as continuous or catastrophic process, and the advantages of using a flexible spectral cut-off and abandoning the ultra-relativistic approximation. Furthermore, we show that using analytical approximations to compute energy fluxes can cause the slope reconstruction to fail. Conclusions. Future applications of our spectral cosmic-ray model in large-scale, full-physics cosmological simulations will represent an important step towards building a robust and observationally verifiable link between the microphysical and macrophysical aspects of the CR component in the modern paradigm of galaxy evolution.

</details>


### [105] [Black-Hole Spin Measurements from X-ray Reflection Spectroscopy: Quality Criteria and Community Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14368)
*Javier A. Garcia,Riley Connors,Laura W. Brenneman,James F. Steiner*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套用于评估X射线反射谱学测量黑洞自旋结果可靠性的实用框架，基于可探测性、唯一性和稳健性三大原则，并建立了质量分类方案和报告检查表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线反射谱学是测量吸积黑洞自旋的关键电磁方法，但现有测量结果受到观测和建模系统误差的影响，缺乏统一的可靠性评估标准。为建立社区可维护的黑洞自旋测量可靠数据集，亟需一个可复现的评估框架。

Method: 基于2025年Wake Forest研讨会讨论，提出三大原则：可探测性（要求存在明确相对论反射信号）、唯一性（要求相对论成分能与连续谱、远距离反射、吸收和仪器效应区分）、稳健性（要求推断的自旋对模型假设、数据选择和吸积态处理方式的变化保持稳定）。据此建立评估标准、质量分类方案和报告检查表，并通过模拟进行标定。

Result: 形成了一套包含评估标准、质量分类方案（稳健/临时/不可评估）和未来研究报告检查表的实用框架，标定工作将在后续论文中完成。

Conclusion: 该框架旨在为高吞吐量、高分辨率X射线天文时代建立一条可复现的路径，以汇编社区维护的可靠黑洞自旋测量结果。

Abstract: X-ray reflection spectroscopy provides one of the most powerful electromagnetic methods for measuring the dimensionless spin of accreting black holes. It has yielded spin constraints for stellar-mass black holes in X-ray binaries and supermassive black holes in active galactic nuclei, and is central to the science goals of current and future X-ray observatories. However, the technique is subject to observational and modeling systematics, including continuum-reflection degeneracy, limited spectral coverage, unresolved distant reflection or absorption, detector effects, source variability, accretion-state dependence, and assumptions inherent to reflection models. Motivated by discussions at the 2025 Wake Forest workshop *Recent Progress on Black Hole Spin Measurements Across the Electromagnetic and Gravitational Spectra*, we propose a practical framework for evaluating whether published reflection-based spin measurements should be considered robust, provisional, or not assessable from the available information. The framework is built on three principles: **detectability**, requiring an unambiguous relativistic reflection signal; **uniqueness**, requiring that the relativistic component be distinguishable from the continuum, distant reflection, absorption, and instrumental effects; and **robustness**, requiring that the inferred spin remain stable against reasonable changes in model assumptions, data selection, and accretion-state treatment. We translate these principles into assessment criteria, a quality-classification scheme, and a reporting checklist for future studies. Calibration of these criteria through dedicated simulations is outlined here and deferred to a companion paper. Our goal is to establish a reproducible path toward a community-maintained compilation of reliable black hole spin measurements for the high-throughput, high-resolution era of X-ray astronomy.

</details>


### [106] [Temporal Memory in Repeating Fast Radio Bursts: Epsilon-Machine Reconstruction of Causal Structure in Burst Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14421)
*Tom Kimpson,Joseph O'Leary*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文首次将计算力学中的ε-机器重构方法应用于快速射电暴（FRB）的等待时间序列，发现FRB 20121102A和FRB 20201124A的爆发具有约1比特的时间记忆（显著区别于无记忆过程），而FRB 20220912A则与无记忆爆发一致。对于前两个重复暴，其记忆源于不同观测会话间活跃率状态的切换，而非会话内部的预测性结构。CHIME的短时过境观测可能抑制了类似结构。该结果为重复FRB的物理机制提供了模型无关的约束：至少两个重复暴的爆发并非无记忆，而是受隐藏状态调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRB的辐射机制未知，判断重复暴的爆发是随机到达还是具有结构化序列是理解其机制的关键约束。现有方法难以从随机过程中提取最小预测模型，因此需要一种模型无关的工具来揭示爆发序列中蕴含的统计记忆结构。

Method: 运用计算力学中的ε-机器重构方法，对三个重复FRB（FAST观测的FRB 20121102A和FRB 20201124A，CHIME观测的FRB 20220912A）的等待时间序列进行分析，计算统计复杂度Cμ（最优预测所需的最小比特数），并通过置换检验（排列替代数据、会话打乱）进行显著性评估，同时模拟CHIME的窗口效应对结构可探测性的影响。

Result: FAST观测的两个重复暴的等待时间序列具有约1比特的显著统计记忆（p≤0.01），而CHIME观测的FRB 20220912A与无记忆过程一致。FRB 20121102A的记忆跨越数小时至数周，横跨39个会话，且会话顺序本身具有预测性（p=0.02）；FRB 20201124A的记忆跨越数小时至数天，跨4个会话，但其信号反映的是会话间的异质性而非顺序。两个源均未表现出会话内部的可辩护预测性记忆。模拟实验表明CHIME的短时过境观测会抑制类似FAST数据中的结构。

Conclusion: 重复FRB中至少有两个（FRB 20121102A和FRB 20201124A）的爆发并非无记忆过程，而是受隐藏状态调控，该状态在不同观测会话间呈现不同的活跃率。任何可行的物理模型都必须复现这一行为。ε-机器重构方法为天体瞬变源的时间序列分析提供了全新的模型无关工具。

Abstract: The emission mechanism of fast radio bursts (FRBs) remains unknown. Whether the bursts from a repeating FRB arrive at random or in a structured sequence is a key constraint on that mechanism. We apply $\varepsilon$-machine reconstruction, a tool from computational mechanics that infers the minimal model capturing all predictive information in a stochastic process. Applied to the waiting-time sequences of three repeating FRBs (FRB~20121102A and FRB~20201124A from FAST; FRB~20220912A from CHIME), the method yields the statistical complexity $C_μ$, the minimum number of bits required for optimal prediction. Both FAST sources carry roughly one bit of temporal memory (significant against permutation surrogates, $p \leq 0.01$; per-source false-discovery-rate-adjusted $p \leq 0.028$), while FRB~20220912A is consistent with memoryless emission. FRB~20201124A's memory spans hours-to-days across four sessions, FRB~20121102A's spans hours-to-weeks across thirty-nine, and neither source shows defensible within-session predictive memory. For FRB~20121102A the ordering of those sessions is itself predictive (session-shuffle $p = 0.02$), whereas FRB~20201124A's signal reflects the contrast between heterogeneous sessions rather than their order. A simulated windowing test shows that CHIME's short transit observations would suppress comparable structure in the FAST data, leaving FRB~20220912A's null result ambiguous. This first application of $\varepsilon$-machine reconstruction to astrophysical transients yields a model-independent constraint: the bursting of at least two of these repeaters is not memoryless, but is governed by a hidden state that occupies distinct activity-rate regimes varying across observing sessions, behaviour that any viable physical model must reproduce.

</details>


### [107] [Cosmogenic photon fluxes at ultra-high energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14670)
*Marcus Niechciol,Chiara Jane Papior,Markus Risse*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了超高能宇宙射线传播过程中产生的宇宙源光子，分析了不同注入参数（如能谱、成分、源距离）对光子产额和通量的影响，并评估了不同源场景下光子通量是否达到现有实验探测极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高能宇宙射线在传播过程中会产生宇宙源光子，但其预期通量水平因注入参数（如能谱、成分、源距离）的不同而可能相差数个量级，因此需要系统研究这些参数对光子产额的影响，以确定哪些场景可能被现有或未来的实验探测到。

Method: 通过理论计算和模拟，在不同注入参数假设下研究光子产额；考虑混合成分场景（基于Pierre Auger天文台数据）以及纯质子或纯铁基准场景，分别确定"最大"和"最小"光子通量。

Result: 光子产额在质子发射源距离约15 Mpc时最大；质子光子产额始终高于同能量重核，但差距随源距离增大而缩小；混合成分场景（无初始质子）的光子通量低于现有实验限值1.5个数量级以上；高能端有显著质子成分的场景可能被探测到；部分纯质子场景已被现有光子限值约束。

Conclusion: 宇宙源光子通量对注入参数高度敏感，混合成分场景若无初始质子则难以被现有实验探测，而纯质子或高能质子成分丰富的场景则可能接近或已被探测限值约束。

Abstract: During their propagation, ultra-high energy cosmic rays produce cosmogenic photons. The expected flux level of these photons may vary by orders of magnitude depending on parameters such as the spectrum and composition of cosmic rays at injection or the source distance. We investigate the photon yields for various assumptions on injection parameters. The photon yield is largest for proton-emitting sources at about 15 Mpc distance for cosmogenic photons above $10^{18}$ eV. While the photon yield from protons always exceeds the one from heavier nuclei of the same total energy, the differences are reduced for larger source distances. Then, we quantify the cosmogenic photon fluxes for different source scenarios. We regard mixed-composition scenarios that were found to provide a reasonable description of the data from the Pierre Auger Observatory. In addition, benchmark scenarios leading to comparably high ("maximum") or low ("minimum") photon fluxes are determined assuming pure proton or pure iron primaries. For the mixed-composition scenarios that do not contain initial protons, the predicted cosmogenic photon fluxes are below present experimental limits by more than 1.5 orders of magnitude. In case of a substantial fraction of protons at the highest energies, the related photon flux might be in reach. Certain parameter combinations of pure proton scenarios are constrained already by present photon limits.

</details>


### [108] [Early Near-Infrared Excess and Rapid Disk-Corona Evolution in the Tidal Disruption Event 2024aepd](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14696)
*Yongxin Wu,Yanan Wang,Thomas M. Reynolds,Shuyuan Wei,Shiyan Zhong,Zikun Lin,Megan Newsome,Sebastian Gomez,Iair Arcavi,Panos Charalampopoulos,Chun Chen,Rongfeng Shen,Ning-Chen Sun,David Aguado,Ismael Pérez-Fournon,Frédérick Poidevin,Zhongnan Dong,Niu Li,Weijian Guo,Hu Zou,Jingbo Sun,Nieves Castro-Rodríguez,Antonio Cabrera-Lavers,Ning Jiang,Hengxiao Guo,J. P. Anderson,Tomás E. Müller-Bravo,Seppo Mattila,Claudia P. Gutiérrez,Amit Kumar,G. Pignata,Xiangkun Liu,R. Dastidar,Brajesh Kumar,Xiaowei Liu,Bin Ma,M. Dennefeld,Francesca Onori,Lydia Makrygianni,Mariusz Gromadzki,Xuan Fang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报道了潮汐瓦解事件（TDE）2024aepd的多波段观测，重点分析了其X射线谱从热盘主导到幂律主导的演化、早期近红外（NIR）超额的发现及其可能的自由-自由辐射起源，以及UV-光學-NIR转折频率的蓝移现象。该事件是第三个被报道具有早期NIR超额的TDE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过多波段观测揭示TDE 2024aepd的物理演化过程，特别是X射线日冕的快速出现与增强、早期NIR超额的起源机制，以及探讨此类NIR超额在TDE群体中的普遍性。

Method: 对TDE 2024aepd进行从X射线到NIR的多波段时序观测，分析其光谱演化（热盘→幂律谱）、NIR超额的光谱特征（近平坦幂律谱）并与已知TDE（AT2019azh、TDE 2025abcr）进行对比。

Result: X射线谱从热盘主导（~178天前）演化为幂律主导并逐步硬化，表明热日冕快速形成；NIR超额在~40天出现，其平坦幂律谱不符合尘埃回波模型，更可能来自光球层包层的自由-自由辐射；UV-光學-NIR转折频率随密度轮廓指数不变而蓝移，表明再处理条件演化而密度结构大致恒定。

Conclusion: TDE 2024aepd是第三个被报道具有早期NIR超额的TDE，支持自由-自由辐射起源；需要更大样本的早期NIR观测来确定此类超额在TDE中的普遍性。

Abstract: We present multi-wavelength observations of the tidal disruption event (TDE) 2024aepd, spanning primarily the first $\sim$300 days after discovery. The X-ray spectrum is initially dominated by a thermal disk component accompanied by a hard excess. From $\sim$178 days onward, the spectrum becomes power-law dominated and subsequently hardens, indicating the rapid emergence and strengthening of a hot corona. A prominent near-infrared (NIR) excess is detected as early as $\sim40$ days. Its nearly flat power-law spectrum strongly deviates from the Rayleigh-Jeans tail of the UV-optical blackbody. Although a conventional dust-echo origin cannot be completely ruled out, free-free emission from a reprocessing photospheric envelope provides a more plausible explanation. Moreover, the UV-optical-to-NIR break shifts to higher frequencies as the density-profile index remains nearly constant, implying evolving reprocessing conditions within a broadly unchanged density structure. Together with AT2019azh and TDE 2025abcr, TDE 2024aepd is the third TDE reported to exhibit an early-time NIR excess. A larger sample with early-time NIR coverage is needed to determine whether such excesses are common among TDEs.

</details>


### [109] [SPH methods in the modelling of compact objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14828)
*Stephan Rosswog*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于光滑粒子流体动力学（SPH）方法的致密天体模拟现状，涵盖牛顿力学和理想气体动力学的SPH基础与最新进展、广义相对论SPH（含全数值相对论）、以及白矮星-白矮星、中子星-中子星、中子星-黑洞系统的引力波驱动并合模拟，并简要提及高恒星密度环境中的动力学碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理和总结SPH方法在致密天体模拟（尤其是引力波驱动并合）中的发展现状与技术进展，为相关领域研究者提供全面的方法学参考。

Method: 本文以综述形式组织内容：(1) 介绍牛顿框架下SPH的基础方法、无网格导数及激波处理等最新进展；(2) 阐述广义相对论SPH（含特殊相对论极限）以及全数值相对论中物质与动态时空的联合演化；(3) 针对白矮星-白矮星、中子星-中子星、中子星-黑洞三类系统，重点讨论引力波驱动并合，并简述高密度环境下的动力学碰撞。

Result: 系统呈现了SPH方法在致密双星并合模拟中的完整技术图谱，包括牛顿与相对论框架下的方法进展以及三类关键天体物理系统的模拟现状。

Conclusion: SPH方法在致密天体并合模拟中取得了显著进展，已从牛顿理想气体动力学拓展至全数值相对论框架，并成功应用于多种双星系统的引力波驱动并合研究。

Abstract: We review the current status of compact object simulations that are based on the Smoothed Particle Hydrodynamics (SPH) method. The first section of this review is dedicated to SPH as a numerical method for Newtonian, ideal gas dynamics and it should be fairly self-contained. It begins with the basics of the method, but also describes recent advances including various meshless derivatives or methods for treating shocks. A separate chapter summarizes general relativistic SPH, including its special relativistic limit, and it explains in some detail the recent development of full numerical relativity in SPH where matter is evolved together with a dynamical spacetime. The remainder of the review has an astrophysical focus, here we discuss the status of the simulations of white dwarf--white dwarf, neutron star--neutron star and neutron star--black hole systems. For each type of system the emphasis is on gravitational-wave-driven mergers, but we also briefly summarize dynamical collisions that can occur in locations with large stellar densities.

</details>


### [110] [A self-consistent Higgs-portal framework for dark matter--admixed neutron stars: Collider-motivated benchmarks meet multimessenger constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14979)
*Adamu Issifu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文在相对论平均场框架下，通过扩展带有大质量Z′矢量介子的希格斯-门户模型，自洽地研究了暗物质掺杂中子星。新模型通过全局粒子分数F_χ表征暗物质含量，避免了外部预设暗物质费米动量的限制，并推导了绝热声速等热力学量的精确解析表达式。研究表明，增加F_χ会软化核物质状态方程、降低最大中子星质量（最多约29%）、增加恒星致密性，且重子-暗物质相互作用以Z′矢量道的排斥为主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单流体暗物质-中子星模型需要外部预设暗物质费米动量，缺乏重子物质与暗物质之间自洽的动力学耦合机制，亟需建立更严格的理论框架以连接粒子物理模型与多信使观测。

Method: 采用相对论平均场框架，扩展希格斯-门户模型引入大质量Z′矢量中介子，实现重子物质与暗物质的密度依赖排斥相互作用。暗物质含量由全局分数F_χ=N_χ/N_B表征，局域暗物质密度自洽确定为n_χ=F_χn_B，并使用NL3ωρ、DD2和FSU2R三种状态方程进行数值计算。

Result: 增加F_χ系统地软化核状态方程，最大中子星质量降低约29%，恒星致密性增加；推导了绝热声速、密度导数及绝热指数的精确解析表达式；微观层面显示重子-暗物质相互作用以Z′矢量道的排斥作用为主导。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了对撞机驱动的WIMP模型与中子星多信使现象学之间的直接联系，对未来的引力波和X射线观测具有潜在意义，并为评估暗物质掺杂状态方程的因果性和热力学稳定性提供了严格的基准。

Abstract: We investigate dark matter (DM)-admixed neutron stars (NSs) within a self-consistent single-fluid relativistic mean-field framework by extending the Higgs-portal model with a massive $Z^\prime$ vector mediator. The resulting density-dependent repulsive interaction dynamically couples the baryonic matter (BM) and DM sectors, allowing the DM content to be characterized by the global particle fraction, $F_χ=N_χ/N_B$, with the local DM density determined self-consistently as $n_χ=F_χn_B$, thereby eliminating the need for the externally prescribed DM Fermi momentum adopted in previous single-fluid models. Using the NL3$ωρ$, DD2, and FSU2R EOSs, we show that increasing $F_χ$ systematically softens the nuclear equation of state (EOS), reduces the maximum NS mass by up to $\sim29\%$, increases stellar compactness, and modifies the thermodynamic response of dense matter. We further derive exact analytical expressions for the adiabatic speed of sound, its density derivative, and the adiabatic index, providing a rigorous benchmark for assessing the causality and thermodynamic stability of DM-admixed EOSs. At the microscopic level, the BM--DM interaction is shown to be dominated by the repulsive $Z^\prime$ vector channel. Our framework establishes a direct connection between collider-motivated WIMP models and the multimessenger phenomenology of NSs, with potential implications for future gravitational-wave and X-ray observations.

</details>


### [111] [Testing the Isotropy of the Universe with the CHIME/FRB Catalog I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14982)
*Jun-Yi Shen,Yuan-Chuan Zou*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用CHIME/FRB目录中536个快速射电暴，通过2PACF和sigma-map两种方法检验宇宙各向同性，结果与各向同性预期一致，但受限于样本量小和协方差矩阵病态等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验宇宙是否在宏观尺度上保持各向同性，这对标准宇宙学模型至关重要。

Method: 使用两点角相关函数（2PACF）和sigma-map方法，并利用CHIME注入数据校正观测偏差。

Result: 两种分析方法均与各向同性预期一致；2PACF存在协方差矩阵病态问题，sigma-map角功率谱未发现显著方向各向异性但低多极不确定性大。

Conclusion: 当前FRB样本支持宇宙各向同性假设，但统计能力有限，未来更大样本的FRB巡天有望改善这些局限。

Abstract: We test the isotropy of the Universe using 536 Fast Radio Bursts (FRBs) from the first CHIME/FRB catalog, employing two complementary statistical methods: the two-point angular correlation function (2PACF) and the sigma-map method, with observational biases corrected using CHIME injection data. Both analyses are consistent with the isotropic expectation, but are limited by the small sample size. The 2PACF suffers from an ill-conditioned covariance matrix, preventing robust parameter inference. The sigma-map angular power spectrum shows no statistically significant directional anisotropy, though the uncertainties at low multipoles remain large. Future FRB surveys are expected to improve these limitations.

</details>


### [112] [PeV extended emission around Galactic PeVatrons and echos of their flares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15031)
*A. Neronov,Ie. Vovk*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文认为银河系PeV伽马射线源周围通常存在多度尺度的扩展发射，这是PeV伽马射线与微波光子相互作用产生电子-正电子对注入星际介质所致。此类发射的面亮度可能因源PeV发射功率的变化而随时间变化，源耀斑会在扩展发射区产生可探测的"回声"，时间尺度从数十年到数千年。作者提出天鹅座区域的超扩展PeV源可能是由天鹅座X-3 PeV伽马射线源产生，或是其过去耀斑的回声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索银河系PeV伽马射线源周围扩展发射的起源及其时间变异性，特别是解释天鹅座区域观测到的超扩展PeV源的可能成因。

Method: 理论分析：基于PeV伽马射线与微波光子相互作用产生电子-正电子对的物理机制，推导扩展发射的面亮度随时间变化的特性，并定量估算耀斑回声的时空尺度。

Result: 发现天鹅座区域的超扩展PeV源可能是天鹅座X-3源过去耀斑产生的回声，其时间尺度覆盖数十年至数千年。

Conclusion: 扩展发射的变异性可作为研究PeV源历史活动的探针，天鹅座X-3的耀斑回声假说为理解银河系PeV天体物理提供了新视角。

Abstract: We notice that Galactic sources of PeV gamma-rays are generally expected to be surrounded by multi-degree-scale extended emission produced as a result of injection of electron-positron pairs in the interstellar medium from interactions of PeV gamma-rays with microwave photons. The surface brightness of such emission may be variable in time because of variability of the PeV emission power of its parent source. A flare of the parent source produces an ``echo'' spreading across the extended emission region that may be detectable on the time scales from decades to millennia. We consider a possibility that the very extended PeV source in the Cygnus region is the produced by the Cygnus X-3 PeV gamma-ray source, possibly being an echo of its past flare.

</details>


### [113] [Swift gives a new BAT-GLIMPSE: Gamma-ray Localization using Imaging and Mosaic techniques for Pointing and Slew Epochs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15130)
*S. Ronchini,T. Parsotan,J. DeLaunay,J. A. Kennea*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文介绍BAT-GLIMPSE，一个全自动开源管道，用于在Swift-BAT数据中低延迟定位瞬态伽马射线源。该管道结合编码掩模成像与镶嵌技术，能够在指向观测和航天器转动期间进行定位搜索。在66个GRB样本上验证，成功恢复了43个事件的弧分级位置，典型偏移≤5角分。在LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA第四次观测运行中，该管道在搜索引力波伽马射线对应体方面发挥了关键作用，与NITRATES协同工作，预计将Swift-BAT的船上弧分定位率提高一倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Swift-BAT在航天器转动期间船载触发被禁用，导致该时段内无法自主检测和定位瞬变源，存在关键观测空白。

Method: 开发全自动开源管道BAT-GLIMPSE，利用BatAnalysis包结合编码掩模成像与镶嵌技术，根据航天器姿态自动选择适当分析策略，实现指向观测和转动期间的定位搜索。

Result: 在66个GUANO通报的GRB样本中，成功恢复43个事件的弧分级位置，与已有定位一致，典型偏移≤5角分；约88%的转动期间GRB通过成像或镶嵌分析被恢复。在LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA O4运行中成功用于搜索引力波伽马射线对应体。

Conclusion: BAT-GLIMPSE填补了Swift-BAT在转动期间的定位空白，与NITRATES协同作用预计将船载弧分定位率提升一倍，充分释放Swift任务在时域和多信使天体物理学中的潜力。

Abstract: The Burst Alert Telescope (BAT) onboard the Neil Gehrels Swift Observatory is capable of localizing gamma-ray transients with arcminute precision, enabling rapid multi-wavelength follow-up. However, onboard triggering is disabled during spacecraft slews, preventing the autonomous detection and localization of transients occurring during these intervals. We present here BAT-GLIMPSE, a fully autonomous, open-source pipeline for the low-latency localization of transient gamma-ray sources in Swift-BAT data. Making use of the BatAnalysis package, the pipeline combines coded-mask imaging and mosaic techniques, automatically selecting the appropriate analysis according to the spacecraft attitude and enabling localization searches during both pointing observations and spacecraft slews. We validate the performance of BAT-GLIMPSE on a sample of 66 GRBs reported in GUANO circulars. The pipeline successfully recovers arcminute positions for 43 events, consistent with published localizations, with typical offsets of $\lesssim5$ arcminutes. Approximately $88\%$ of the GRBs occurring during spacecraft slews are recovered through imaging or mosaic analyses. During the fourth LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA observing run, the role of BAT-GLIMPSE was crucial in the search for gamma-ray counterparts of gravitational waves, particularly in response to pre-merger alerts which triggered the slew of the Swift spacecraft with extremely low latency. Operating synergistically with NITRATES, BAT-GLIMPSE fills the critical gap left by slew intervals and, together, the two pipelines are estimated to double the onboard arcminute-localization rate of Swift-BAT, unlocking the full potential of the Swift mission for time-domain and multi-messenger astrophysics.

</details>


### [114] [Spectral study of the outburst decay of the accreting millisecond X-ray pulsar SRGA J144459.2-604207](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15167)
*Amar Deo Chandra*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究利用NICER和Swift观测数据，分析了吸积X射线毫秒脉冲星SRGA J144459.2-604207在2024年爆发期间及之后的能谱演化，发现宁静态可用幂律或黑体模型拟合，长期宁静光度可用深地壳加热解释，爆发衰减中检测到多次再耀发，其中一次与超快外流和射电辐射近乎同时发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解吸积X射线毫秒脉冲星在爆发衰减、再耀发和宁静态的光谱特性，探究其长期宁静X射线活动的物理机制，以及外流、射电辐射和喷流形成的可能机制。

Method: 使用NICER和Swift观测数据，采用吸收康普顿化模型拟合爆发衰减、再耀发和宁静态光谱；宁静态还使用吸收幂律和吸收黑体模型拟合；通过深地壳加热模型解释长期宁静光度；在射电-X射线光度图上比较源的位置；估算螺旋桨光度并与再耀发和宁静态光度比较。

Result: 宁静态光谱可用吸收幂律和黑体模型解释；长期宁静光度可用深地壳加热模型解释；伴星日冕活动无法单独提供宁静X射线光度；螺旋桨光度小于2024年爆发再耀发和宁静态光度；爆发衰减中检测到多次再耀发，其中一次与超快外流和射电辐射近乎同时。

Conclusion: SRGA J144459.2-604207在2024年爆发的光谱演化得到表征，其长期宁静活动主要源于深地壳加热，而非伴星日冕活动；再耀发中探测到的超快外流和射电辐射暗示了外流、射电辐射与喷流形成的关联机制。

Abstract: We study the spectra of the accreting X-ray millisecond pulsar SRGA J144459.2-604207 during the 2024 outburst using the Neutron star Interior Composition Explorer (NICER) and Swift observations. The spectra during the outburst decay, reflares, and quiescent state are explored using the absorbed Comptonized model. We find that the spectra during the quiescent state can also be explained using the absorbed power-law and absorbed blackbody model. The spectral evolution of the source is explored as the outburst decays into quiescence. We study the long-term quiescent X-ray activity of the source spanning roughly 45 years and find that the long-term quiescent luminosity may be explained using the deep crustal heating model. We also find that the coronal activity of the companion star alone cannot power the quiescent X-ray luminosity of the source. We place the source on the radio-X-ray luminosity plane and compare its position with other sources. We estimate the propeller luminosity of the source and find that it is smaller than the estimated luminosity during reflares and the quiescent state during the 2024 outburst. Several reflares are detected during the outburst decay, one of which is near-simultaneous with the detection of an ultrafast outflow and radio emission. We explore plausible mechanisms that may power outflow, radio emission and associated jet formation in this accreting binary.

</details>


### [115] [When will T Coronae Borealis next erupt as a nova? Constraints from recurrence, orbital phase, and accretion-state evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15245)
*Songpeng Pei,Xiaowan Zhang,Renzhi Su,Ziwei Ou,Yongzhi Cai,Qiang Li,Yu Liu,Xiaoqin Ren,Taozhi Yang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: T CrB是一颗最邻近的共生再发新星。通过历史复发、轨道相位和吸积态演化三个约束条件，论文给出了条件性监测窗口而非唯一爆发日期。若2026年亮度下降模拟1946年前兆，2026年12月爆发可能；若亮度保持低位，吸积亏损估算给出最早下限在2029年5月前后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T CrB是距离最近且被密集监测的共生再发新星，迫切需要对其下一次爆发的时点进行科学约束和预测分析。

Method: 综合历史复发间隔（三组有效周期）、轨道相位（四组历史爆发相位，构成φ≈0.44和φ≈0.62两对窗口）以及2024年后吸积态演化（含1946年前兆下降对比和吸积亏损估算），进行条件性概率分析和诊断场景推演。

Result: 以2026年7月11日未爆发为条件，三组历史间隔给出2026年剩余时间30.2%、随后一年56.9%的生存条件概率；两种场景（类似1946年前兆下降 vs. 高态亮度持续偏低）分别指向2026年12月或2029年5月以后。

Conclusion: 论文不提供唯一爆发日期预测，而是输出条件性监测窗口和诊断性场景；最终爆发时间将用于检验各假设的物理合理性。

Abstract: T Coronae Borealis (T CrB) is the nearest symbiotic recurrent nova and is now intensively monitored for its next eruption. We combine three constraints on the eruption time: historical recurrence, orbital phase, and recent accretion-state evolution. Conditioning on no eruption by 2026 July 11, the three effective historical intervals give illustrative survival-conditioned probabilities of 30.2\% for the rest of 2026 and 56.9\% within the following year; these are empirical indicators, not physical prediction probabilities. The four adopted historical eruption phases do not select a unique ignition phase, but form two loose pairs near $φ\simeq0.44$ and $φ\simeq0.62$, used here only as monitoring windows. The 1946 pre-eruption dip is difficult to explain by either a pure accretion-rate decline or standard dust extinction, and may have involved both accretion restructuring and source-dependent obscuration. If the renewed 2026 decline is the true pre-1946 analogue, an eruption around 2026 December remains plausible. Conversely, if post-2024 brightness remains below the 2014--2023 high state, an accretion-deficit estimate gives an earliest lower limit near 2029 May. The data therefore support conditional monitoring windows, not a unique date. These are conditional diagnostic scenarios rather than competing point predictions; the eventual eruption epoch will test their underlying assumptions.

</details>

### [116] [Analysis of Public Schools Educational Performance Based on Causal Models and Hierarchical Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14124)
*Anderson L. de Paula,Pedro C. dos Santos,Renato A. Krohling*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用巴西学校普查和Saeb数据，通过层次聚类识别学校类型学，并采用因果分析方法探究学校特征与教育表现之间的因果关系，发现不同学校类型在平均表现上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模教育数据集日益可用，但学校的制度异质性和教育数据的结构复杂性给传统统计建模带来了挑战，需要更有效的方法来理解学校特征与表现之间的关系。

Method: 对巴西学校普查和Saeb数据进行预处理和标准化，应用层次聚类识别具有相似结构特征的学校群体，随后采用因果分析技术探究学校特征与教育结果之间的潜在因果关系。

Result: 发现了不同的学校类型学，不同学校类型在平均表现上存在统计显著的差异；因果分析揭示了可能影响教育表现的结构性和情境性因素。

Conclusion: 不同学校存在可区分的结构特征类型，这些特征与教育表现存在因果关系，有助于理解学校有效性的内在机制。

Abstract: The increasing availability of large-scale educational datasets has expanded the use of quantitative methods for investigating school performance. However, institutional heterogeneity among schools and the structural complexity of educational data pose substantial challenges to traditional statistical modeling approaches. This study investigates the existence of school typologies based on structural, pedagogical, and demographic characteristics, and examines how these typologies relate to performance in the Brazilian Basic Education Assessment System (Saeb). Using data from the Brazilian School Census and Saeb, data preprocessing and normalization procedures are applied followed by hierarchical clustering to identify groups of schools with similar structural profiles. After the identification of these typologies, causal analysis techniques are employed to investigate potential causal relationships between school characteristics and educational outcomes. The results reveal the presence of distinct school profiles and statistically significant differences in average performance among them. The causal analysis provides insights into the structural and contextual factors that may influence educational performance, contributing to a better understanding of the mechanisms associated with school effectiveness.

</details>


### [117] [Optimal Design for Generalized Progressive Hybrid Censored Data via Constrained, Unconstrained, Compound, and Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14926)
*Rathin Das,Tanmay Sen,Deepak Prajapati*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究I型广义渐进混合删失方案的最优设计，通过建立成本约束优化框架、提出多目标优化模型及变邻域搜索算法，同时优化A-最优性准则和实验成本，并引入香农微分熵作为信息论补充度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寿命试验中，传统的删失方案设计往往单独考虑统计效率或实验成本，缺乏同时优化多个目标的系统性框架。本文旨在解决I型广义渐进混合删失方案中检测时间、保证失效数和渐进删失方案的联合最优设计问题，以平衡估计精度与实验成本之间的矛盾。

Method: 1) 建立成本约束优化框架；2) 分析A-最优性准则与实验成本关于检测时间和保证失效数的结构性质，揭示冲突行为并开发高效搜索算法；3) 构建多目标优化模型；4) 提出变邻域搜索(VNS)算法确定最优渐进移除向量；5) 推导香农微分熵作为信息论补充度量。

Result: A-最优性准则与实验成本存在冲突行为，据此开发的搜索算法显著降低计算负担；VNS算法能有效探索可行设计空间避免穷举；折衷设计方案同时改善估计精度和降低实验成本；熵最优设计与A-最优设计通常不同，表明香农熵刻画观测数据的不确定性而非估计精度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法论为最优寿命试验设计提供了高效计算框架，为未来融合统计效率、实验成本和信息论不确定性的多目标优化奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the optimal design of Type-I generalized progressive hybrid censoring schemes for life-testing experiments. The design problem involves simultaneously determining the inspection time, the guaranteed number of failures, and the progressive censoring scheme. First we develop a cost-constrained optimization framework for determining the optimal censoring scheme. Structural properties of the A-optimality criterion and the experimental cost with respect to the inspection time and the guaranteed number of failures are established. It reveals that they are conflicting behaviors which enables to develop an efficient search algorithm that substantially reduces the computational burden. Building on these theoretical results, a multi-objective optimization model is proposed to simultaneously minimize A-optimality criterion and the experimental cost. A Variable Neighborhood Search (VNS) algorithm is proposed to efficiently determine the optimal progressive removal vector by exploring the feasible design space while avoiding exhaustive enumeration. The resulting compromise designs simultaneously improve estimation precision and reduce experimental cost. In addition, the Shannon differential entropy of the observed lifetime distribution is derived and employed as a complementary information-theoretic measure for evaluating the selected censoring schemes. Numerical studies show that entropy-optimal designs generally differ from A-optimal designs, indicating that Shannon entropy characterizes uncertainty in the observed data rather than estimation precision. The proposed methodology provides an efficient computational framework for optimal life-test design and offers a foundation for future multi-objective optimization incorporating statistical efficiency, experimental cost, and information-theoretic uncertainty.

</details>


### [118] [Statistical Modelling of Planetary Boundary Layer Height and Its Measurement Uncertainty Using GRUAN Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14960)
*Tommaso Locatelli,Alessandro Fasso,Fabio Madonna*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从探空仪观测不确定性传播到行星边界层高度(PBLH)估计的通用方法，该方法结合GRUAN数据产品、状态空间模型和蒙特卡洛模拟，不仅量化了PBLH的不确定性，还在某些情形下提高了PBLH检测的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PBLH是气象和空气质量应用中的关键变量，但现有从观测推导PBLH的方法很少量化其相关的不确定性，导致对PBLH估计的可靠性缺乏认识。

Method: 三部分框架：(1)使用GRUAN数据产品提供可追溯的不确定性估计；(2)利用状态空间模型生成符合观测及其不确定性的物理合理模拟垂直廓线；(3)采用蒙特卡洛方法将测量不确定性传播到PBLH估计中，细化反演并量化不确定性。

Result: 在多个案例研究中，该方法不仅提供了PBLH的不确定性估计，还初步显示出PBLH检测鲁棒性提升的迹象，尤其是在标准梯度方法对测量不确定性敏感的情景下。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效量化PBLH估计的不确定性，并在某些情况下提高检测稳健性，为气象和空气质量应用提供更可靠的PBLH信息。

Abstract: The Planetary Boundary Layer (PBL) governs the exchange of energy and moisture and hosts the highest concentrations of pollutants before they mix into the free troposphere. The height of the PBL (PBLH) is therefore a key variable in meteorological and air-quality applications. Despite the wide range of methods available to derive PBLH from atmospheric observations, the associated uncertainties are rarely quantified. This study presents a methodology for propagating radiosonde measurement uncertainty into PBLH estimates obtained from state-of-the-art retrieval methods, including the parcel method, gradient-based methods, and the Richardson-number method. The framework relies on three components. First, it uses the GCOS Reference Upper-Air Network (GRUAN) Data Product, which provides traceable uncertainty estimates for all variables required in PBLH retrievals. Second, it employs a state-space model that captures the structure of atmospheric profiles and enables the generation of physically plausible simulated vertical profiles consistent with both observations and their uncertainties. Third, a Monte Carlo approach is used to propagate measurement uncertainty into the PBLH estimates, refining the retrieval and quantifying its uncertainty. Beyond providing uncertainty estimates, the methodology also shows preliminary signs of increased robustness in PBLH detection across several case studies, particularly in situations where standard gradient-based methods exhibit sensitivity to measurement uncertainty.

</details>


### [119] [Flood risk estimation via geometric extremal graphical models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15000)
*Kristina Grolmusova,Jeniffer L. Wadsworth,Thordis L. Thorarinsdottir*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用多元几何极值理论，基于块图结构定义规范函数，构建了适用于高维河流网络的极值图形化模型，并应用于英格兰普雷斯顿地区10个站点的洪水极值分析，展示了良好的拟合效果和外推性能，同时引入校正系数改善边缘概率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何框架下的极值方法在处理高维数据（如河流网络中10-20+个测站）时维度受限，而洪水风险评估需要同时分析多个位置的水文极值，因此亟需一种在高维空间中部稀疏且有效的统计模型。

Method: 将几何极值图形模型的理论成果转化为统计实践：利用块图（block graphs）结构定义几何极值中的规范函数（gauge function），构建可以同时处理同步与非同步流量的简单和复杂模型，并引入校正系数修正边缘概率的过估或低估问题。

Result: 模型在英格兰普雷斯顿地区河流网络的10个站点上拟合良好，表现出较强的外推性能，并成功计算了网络中四个位置同时发生洪水的概率。

Conclusion: 基于块图的几何极值图形模型能够有效处理高维河流网络中的洪水极值分析，校正系数的引入进一步提升了边际概率的准确性，为多站点联合洪水风险评估提供了可行工具。

Abstract: We exploit the new framework of multivariate geometric extreme value theory for the statistical analysis of river flow extremes at multiple locations on a river network. Current methodologies within the geometric framework are limited to a relatively low number of dimensions. This is insufficient for the purposes of flood risk estimation, since the number of gauging stations on a river network is often of the order $10-20+$. In order to create a parsimonious model in higher dimensions, we translate recent theoretical work on geometric extremal graphical models into statistical practice. We define the gauge function, a key object in geometric extremes, in a structured way using block graphs, which are a natural way of expressing the river network. We introduce both simple models, and more complex ones that can accommodate both simultaneous and non-simultaneous flows, and apply them to extreme flows at 10 locations on a river network around Preston, in north-west England. The models are shown to fit well and indicate strong extrapolation performance. We also introduce a correction coefficient for the geometric framework to address potential over- or under-estimation of marginal probabilities. The overall utility of our approach is illustrated through calculation of probabilities of simultaneous flooding at four locations on the network.

</details>

### [120] [Tensor Network Methods for Advection-Diffusion-Reaction Systems Using Quantum-Inspired Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14150)
*Nahid Binandeh Dehaghani,Rafal Wisniewski,A. Pedro Aguiar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子启发的张量网络框架，用于求解对流-扩散-反应(ADR)偏微分方程。通过将离散化解场编码为矩阵乘积态(MPS)，微分算子表示为矩阵乘积算子(MPO)，并在张量网络形式下使用显式欧拉更新进行时间积分。在一维和二维ADR问题上的数值实验表明，该方法在多种动力学条件下保持紧凑、稳定和精确，能够以较小的键维数捕获局部解和全局观测量，展示了张量网络作为高效结构保持工具在PDE模拟中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维PDE模拟面临维度灾难问题，传统数值方法在多个空间维度下计算与存储开销呈指数增长，亟需一种能够高效压缩表示解场且保持结构特性的新框架。

Method: 采用量子启发的张量网络方法：将离散化解场编码为矩阵乘积态(MPS)，微分算子表示为矩阵乘积算子(MPO)，时间积分在张量网络形式下通过显式欧拉更新配合可控截断完成。

Result: 在一维和二维ADR问题上的数值实验表明，该方法在多种动力学条件下保持紧凑性、稳定性和精确性，能以较小的键维数同时捕获局部解轮廓和全局观测量。

Conclusion: 张量网络作为高效的结构保持工具，在多个空间维度的PDE模拟中具有显著潜力，能够有效缓解维度灾难问题。

Abstract: We present a quantum-inspired tensor-network framework for solving advection-diffusion-reaction (ADR) partial differential equations. Discretized solution fields are encoded as matrix product states (MPS), while differential operators are represented as matrix product operators (MPOs). Time integration is performed entirely in tensor-network form using explicit Euler updates with controlled truncation. The method is evaluated on one- and two-dimensional ADR problems and compared with high-accuracy Runge-Kutta reference solutions. Numerical results show that the proposed representation remains compact, stable, and accurate across a range of dynamical regimes. The solver captures both local solution profiles and global observables while maintaining small bond dimensions throughout the simulation. These results highlight the potential of tensor networks as efficient structure-preserving tools for PDE simulation in multiple spatial dimensions.

</details>


### [121] [NGMRES convergence analysis and proof of acceleration for contractive and noncontractive iterations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14268)
*Y. He,L. Rebholz,M. Xiao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文首次对非线性GMRES（NGMRES）方法应用于压缩和非压缩不动点迭代（FPI）求解一般非线性系统进行了收敛性分析和加速证明。主要结果表明，在压缩和非压缩情况下，优化问题的比率增益是实现加速（或实现）收敛的机制。分析还揭示了与优化问题相关的第二个重要量，该量直接预测每次迭代的线性收敛速率，并证明其最多为1；因此只有高阶项负责NGMRES的不收敛。数值结果验证了理论，展示了加速如何改进收敛，表明预测线性收敛速率的量非常准确，并且可以用于自适应选择NGMRES深度，展示了重启如何改善非压缩迭代的收敛，展示了NGMRES天然适用于寻找多解PDE的不同解，以及NGMRES在应用于超线性FPI时可能优于Anderson加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为NGMRES方法提供严谨的收敛性分析与加速证明，揭示其加速机制，并指导实际应用中的参数选择。

Method: 基于优化问题的比率增益和线性收敛速率预测量，对压缩和非压缩不动点迭代下的NGMRES进行理论分析。

Result: 证明了比率增益是实现加速的关键；揭示了预测线性收敛速率的量并证明其≤1；数值实验验证了理论准确性，展示了自适应深度选择、重启策略、多解PDE求解及优于Anderson加速的效果。

Conclusion: NGMRES是一种有效的非线性系统求解加速方法，其理论分析为算法改进和应用提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: This paper gives the first convergence analysis and proof of acceleration for nonlinear GMRES (NGMRES) applied to contractive and noncontractive fixed point iterations (FPIs) for solving general nonlinear systems. Our main results are that in both the contractive and noncontractive cases, the ratio gain of the optimization problem is the mechanism responsible for accelerating (or enabling) convergence. Our analysis also reveals a second important quantity related to the optimization problem, which directly predicts the linear convergence rate at each iteration and proves it is at most 1; hence only higher order terms are responsible for NGMRES non-convergence. Numerical results for several challenging nonlinear test problems are given that illustrate the theory, show how the acceleration improves convergence, show that the quantity predicting the linear convergence rate is remarkably accurate and moreover can be useful for adaptively choosing NGMRES depth, show how restarts can improve convergence in noncontractive iterations, show how NGMRES is naturally suited for finding distinct solutions of a multi-solution PDE, and that NGMRES can perform better than Anderson acceleration when applied to superlinear FPIs.

</details>


### [122] [A structure-preserving Numerical Method for the Compressible Resistive-Hall-MHD System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14286)
*Murtazo Nazarov,Rafael Rodriguez-Velasco,Ignacio Tomas*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种保结构的可压缩电阻性Hall-MHD模型数值方法，将算子分裂为欧拉部分（使用连续Lagrange元）和磁部分（使用旋度协调有限元空间），分别保证正性、能量守恒、熵原理和散度约束，并通过多项基准测试验证了方法的鲁棒性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对可压缩电阻性Hall-MHD模型，现有方法难以同时保持物理量的正性、能量守恒、熵原理以及磁场散度约束等多个重要结构性质，需要设计一种保结构的数值格式。

Method: 将微分算子分裂为流体力学部分（可压缩Euler方程，使用连续Lagrange元）和磁部分（Lorentz力与感应方程耦合系统，使用curl-conforming有限元空间）。流体部分使用显式SSP-RK方法，磁部分使用Crank-Nicholson方法（需Newton迭代）。引入高阶人工电阻率改善非线性残差的条件数和Jacobian的可逆性。

Result: 在光滑哨声波、Orszag-Tang涡（比较电阻MHD与电阻Hall-MHD）、磁重联等基准测试中，验证了方法的鲁棒性和准确性。给出了Newton迭代Jacobian的强制性估计。

Conclusion: 该方法能够同时保持密度和内能正性、总能量守恒、比熵最小原理以及磁场散度约束，是一种有效的保结构数值格式。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a structure-preserving method for the compressible resistive Hall-magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) model. The differential operator is split into two parts: a hydrodynamic part consisting of the compressible Euler equations, and a magnetic part consisting of a system coupling the Lorentz force and the induction equation. The method uses continuous Lagrange elements for the Euler part and a curl-conforming finite element space for the magnetic part. The hydrodynamic part preserves the positivity of the density and internal energy, the conservation of total energy, and the minimum principle for the specific entropy. Owing to the choice of finite elements, the magnetic part preserves the divergence involution constraint. The fluid part is solved using explicit strong-stability-preserving Runge-Kutta (SSP-RK) methods, whereas the magnetic part is solved by Crank-Nicholson method, which requires using Newton's method. Coercivity estimates for the Jacobian of the corresponding Newton iteration are presented. We introduce a high-order artificial resistivity to improve the conditioning of the nonlinear residual and the invertibility of the Jacobian. Several challenging benchmarks, including a smooth whistler wave, the Orszag-Tang vortex for comparing resistive MHD with resistive Hall-MHD, and a magnetic reconnection problem, are solved to validate the robustness and accuracy of the method.

</details>


### [123] [Neural Very Weak Formulations enabling Hardware-Oriented deep PDE solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14498)
*Gabriel Acosta,Francisco Bersetche*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最小二乘法的极弱形式椭圆问题神经网络离散化方法，通过使用光滑测试函数来降低对神经网络试函数正则性的要求，避免了自动微分，并在奇异解和高维问题中表现出良好性能，特别关注了适合硬件实现的阶梯激活和单比特量化线性函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的神经网络求解偏微分方程通常需要自动微分来计算损失函数中的导数项，计算成本较高。作者希望探索一种能够避免自动微分、同时允许使用低正则性神经网络作为试函数的求解框架，以提高计算效率并适应硬件实现。

Method: 采用最小二乘极弱形式（least-squares very weak formulation）处理椭圆问题，将微分算子通过分部积分转移到光滑测试函数上，从而降低对神经网络试函数的光滑性要求。测试集在不同的神经网络空间上评估该方法，包括基于阶梯激活函数和单比特量化线性函数的试函数。

Result: 该方法在概念验证性实验中表现良好，成功应用于奇异解问题和高维问题场景。使用低正则性神经网络（如阶梯激活函数）仍能获得有效的离散求解。

Conclusion: 最小二乘极弱形式结合低正则性神经网络是一种可行的策略，既能避免自动微分带来的计算负担，又能推广到具有挑战性的问题设定中。特别地，适合硬件实现的简单激活函数在该框架下同样有效。

Abstract: We show, as a proof of concept, that least-squares very weak formulations of elliptic problems can be effectively discretized by neural networks possessing low regularity, provided the test functions are drawn from appropriately smooth spaces. Apart from the immediate computational benefit of avoiding automatic differentiation, this approach, evaluated across various neural network spaces, demonstrates good performance even in challenging contexts, such as singular solutions and high dimensional settings. Particular attention is paid to trial functions based on step activations and one bit quantized linear functions, which are amenable to efficient hardware-oriented implementations.

</details>


### [124] [Steering dynamic network centrality via control theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14610)
*Fabio Durastante,Beatrice Meini,Luca Saluzzi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对时间演化网络（时序网络）中的动态中心性问题，提出了一种最优控制框架，通过最小化网络结构修改来将节点中心性引导至期望状态。该框架将问题建模为基于常微分方程的最优控制问题，利用庞特里亚金最大值原理求解，并采用Krylov子空间技术处理大规模计算。实验验证了其在合成和真实时序网络上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序网络在生物学、社会科学和信息技术等领域广泛存在，其拓扑和边权重随时间动态变化，直接影响信息流、交通效率和神经活动等过程。理解和控制这些网络对于预测未来行为、优化动态过程至关重要，而动态中心性（衡量节点在时变网络中重要性的指标）的控制问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 将动态中心性控制问题形式化为一个基于常微分方程（矩阵或向量形式）的最优控制问题，控制作用于网络边。利用庞特里亚金最大值原理推导最优解条件。针对大规模问题，采用Krylov子空间技术近似计算矩阵-函数乘积，避免显式构造稠密矩阵函数。

Result: 在合成和真实时序网络上的数值实验表明，所提出的框架能够在给定的控制约束下有效地将接收中心性（receive centrality）引导至期望状态。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架将静态网络中的中心性控制问题推广到时序网络，为动态网络中的节点重要性调控提供了理论基础和高效计算方法，在信息传播、交通调度等动态过程优化中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Time-evolving networks, or temporal networks, play a crucial role in modeling dynamic interactions across various domains, including biology, social sciences, and information technology. Unlike static networks, these systems undergo continuous changes in topology and edge weights, influencing processes such as information flow, transportation efficiency, and neural activity. Understanding and controlling these networks are essential for predicting future behavior and optimizing dynamic processes. This work focuses on the problem of dynamic centrality, a measure of node importance in time-dependent networks. Specifically, we address how to steer network centrality to a desired state by making minimal modifications to the network structure. This problem is formulated as an optimal control problem for an ordinary differential equation, either matrix- or vector-based, where the control acts on network edges. The proposed framework generalizes centrality control problems studied in static networks and leverages the Pontryagin Maximum Principle for efficient solutions. For large-scale problems, the required matrix-function actions are approximated by Krylov-type techniques, avoiding the explicit formation of dense matrix functions. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real temporal networks show that the proposed framework can effectively steer receive centrality under prescribed control constraints.

</details>


### [125] [Gradient-enhanced spline dimensional decomposition for uncertainty quantification with limited training samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14615)
*Eunho Heo,Dongjin Lee*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出梯度增强样条维度分解（GE-SDD），利用函数值和偏导数训练代理模型，通过岭回归和分组K折交叉验证解决数据有限时的病态问题，在多维算例中优于标准SDD和梯度增强Kriging。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准样条维度分解（SDD）在处理高维工程响应时，有限训练数据使系数估计严重病态，需要利用梯度信息提升代理模型的准确性和鲁棒性。

Method: 构建对角行权重矩阵平衡函数和导数块的Frobenius范数；在概率加权Sobolev坐标下通过岭回归求解平衡系统；使用分组K折交叉验证选择正则化参数防止信息泄露；将解映射回L2正交SDD基以保持闭式均值和方差估计。

Result: 在二维连续指数函数、三参数线性动力系统和30维25杆桁架算例中，GE-SDD比标准SDD更准确，比梯度增强Kriging更鲁棒地利用梯度。在非光滑基准上中位NRMSE为1.022%（Kriging为8.731%）；桁架算例中，中等及以上训练规模下NRMSE和标准差估计均优于Kriging。

Conclusion: 梯度增强的效果依赖于输入维度、基函数分辨率、训练样本数量以及目标UQ量，所提方法在不同条件下展现不同优势。

Abstract: A spline dimensional decomposition (SDD) surrogate effectively represents high-dimensional engineering responses with localized features and complex nonlinearities in uncertainty quantification (UQ). However, limited training data can make coefficient estimation from function values severely ill-conditioned. We propose gradient-enhanced SDD (GE-SDD), which trains the surrogate using function values and partial derivatives. A diagonal row-weight matrix balances the function and derivative blocks by their Frobenius norms. We solve the balanced system through ridge regression in probability-weighted Sobolev coordinates and select the regularization parameter using grouped K-fold cross-validation to prevent information leakage. Mapping the solution back to the L2-orthonormal SDD basis preserves closed-form mean and variance estimates. We evaluate the proposed GE-SDD on a two-dimensional continuous exponential function, a linear dynamical system with three uncertain parameters, and a 30-dimensional 25-bar truss. GE-SDD is more accurate than standard SDD and uses gradients more robustly than gradient-enhanced Kriging. GE-SDD achieves a median NRMSE of 1.022% on the nonsmooth benchmark, compared with 8.731% for Kriging. For the truss, GE-SDD yields lower NRMSE and more accurate standard-deviation estimates than Kriging at moderate training sizes and above. Overall, the benefits of gradient augmentation depend on input dimension, basis resolution, training size, and the target UQ quantity.

</details>


### [126] [A new strategy for physics-informed neural networks based on hierarchical collocation point refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14665)
*Minjae Choi,Dukhwan Shin,Youngsoo Yang,Eunjung Lee*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出MPU-PINNs，一种基于多重网格的粗到细训练策略，通过逐步增加训练点数量来降低PINNs求解PDE的计算成本，在多个基准问题上实现了与常规PINNs相当的精度并大幅减少了训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINNs在需要大量配点来精确强制执行控制方程时，训练计算成本高昂，亟需一种更高效的训练策略来提升计算效率和可扩展性。

Method: 提出MPU-PINNs方法：采用粗到细的多重网格训练策略，先在粗配点集上训练网络，然后将学到的参数传递给更精细的层；结合缩放技术缓解高频问题中的谱偏差，逐步增加训练点数从全局特征到局部细节进行学习。

Result: 在二维和三维泊松方程、对流扩散反应方程及亥姆霍兹方程等基准PDE上，MPU-PINNs大幅减少了训练时间，同时达到了与传统PINNs、SA-PINNs和XPINNs相当的精度，粗到细的学习策略显著降低了精细层的优化难度。

Conclusion: MPU-PINNs提供了一种高效的单网络训练框架，增强了PINNs在广泛PDE问题上的计算效率和可扩展性。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) offer a flexible framework for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), but training can become computationally expensive when a large number of collocation points are required to accurately enforce the governing equations. To alleviate this cost, we introduce multigrid-based parameter-updated PINNs (MPU-PINNs), a coarse-to-fine training strategy that progressively increases the number of training points throughout the learning process. The proposed approach begins by training a neural network on a coarse set of collocation points and then transfers the learned parameters to successively finer levels. This initialization strategy enables the network to capture the global features of the solution at a relatively low computational cost before refining local details with additional training points. To further improve performance for high-frequency problems, we incorporate a scaling technique that mitigates the effects of spectral bias during training. We evaluate MPU-PINNs on several benchmark PDEs, including two- and three-dimensional Poisson equations, a convection-diffusion-reaction equation, and the Helmholtz equation. Numerical experiments indicate that MPU-PINNs greatly reduce training time while achieving accuracy comparable to that of conventional PINNs and other representative variants such as SA-PINNs and XPINNs. The results further suggest that the proposed coarse-to-fine learning strategy substantially decreases the optimization effort required at finer levels. Overall, MPU-PINNs provide an efficient single-network training framework that enhances the computational efficiency and scalability of PINNs for a broad range of PDE problems.

</details>


### [127] [Operator-Split Bayesian Learning for Elliptic PDEs with Unequal Interior and Boundary Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14680)
*Emmanuel E. Oguadimma*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种算子分裂贝叶斯学习框架，用于解决内部与边界观测数量不等的二阶椭圆Dirichlet问题。通过为源项和边界值赋予独立贝叶斯神经网络先验，将乘积后验通过椭圆解算子前推，证明了后验收缩到真解，并给出了近最优的收缩率上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常假设内部和边界观测数量相等或平衡，但实际应用中两者往往不匹配。需要一种理论严谨的框架来处理这种观测不平衡问题，并给出最优的收敛保证。

Method: 采用算子分裂贝叶斯学习框架：分别对源项和边界值赋予独立的BNN先验，形成乘积后验，再通过椭圆解算子前推得到解的后验分布。理论分析后验收缩率，数值实验验证不确定性传播和采样预算不均衡的影响。

Result: 证明了后验在真解附近收缩，收缩半径由域内贡献（由二阶椭圆算子控制）和边界贡献（由边界本征维度控制）分离构成。结合极小化下界，得到了近极小化最优的上界（差对数因子）。数值实验展示了源和边界不确定性的传播以及采样预算不均衡对后验重建的影响。

Conclusion: 该框架为内部与边界观测数量不等的椭圆反问题提供了理论保证和实用方法，收缩率接近最优。

Abstract: We propose an operator-split Bayesian learning framework for second-order uniformly elliptic Dirichlet problems with unequal numbers of interior and boundary observations. The data consist of noisy measurements of the source in the domain and noisy measurements of the boundary values. Independent Bayesian neural-network (BNN) priors are assigned to these two quantities, and the resulting product posterior is pushed forward through the elliptic solution operator. We prove that the posterior induced by this construction contracts around the true solution. The contraction radius separates a domain contribution, governed by the second-order elliptic operator, from a boundary contribution, governed by the intrinsic dimension of the boundary. Together with the minimax lower bound of \cite{ZhaoLu2026}, this yields a near-minimax upper bound up to logarithmic factors. Our numerical experiments illustrate the propagation of source and boundary uncertainty and the effects of unequal sampling budgets on the posterior reconstruction.

</details>


### [128] [A low-rank hierarchical framework for the non-Markovian stochastic Schrödinger equation with convergence analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14689)
*Zhuohan Zhang,Zhenning Cai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于低秩近似浴相关函数的非马尔可夫随机薛定谔方程数值框架，通过截断层级方程系统在计算效率与物理保真度间取得平衡，并建立了收敛性分析，数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非马尔可夫随机薛定谔方程（NMSSE）的数值求解面临计算复杂度高与物理精度之间的矛盾，现有方法在平衡二者时存在局限，需要一种更灵活、可推广的数值框架。

Method: 将记忆核（memory kernel）分解为有限维低秩表示，并推导出截断的层级方程系统；该框架数学上推广了HOPS方法，将其作为特例包含在内，同时提供更灵活的非马尔可夫效应表示。

Result: 建立了层级框架在温和假设下的严格收敛性分析；通过多个基准模型的数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性和准确性。

Conclusion: 所提出的数值框架在计算可处理性与物理保真度之间取得了有效平衡，且作为HOPS的数学推广，为非马尔可夫量子动力学模拟提供了更通用的求解方案。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a novel numerical framework for the non-Markovian stochastic Schrödinger equation (NMSSE) based on a low-rank approximation of the bath correlation functions. By decomposing the memory kernel into a finite-dimensional representation, we derive a truncated system of hierarchical equations that effectively balances computational tractability with physical fidelity. A rigorous convergence analysis is established for the hierarchical framework under mild assumptions. We demonstrate that our formulation serves as a mathematical generalization of the Hierarchy of Pure States (HOPS), encompassing it as a special case while offering a more flexible representation of non-Markovian effects. Numerical experiments across several benchmark models are presented to illustrate the validity and efficacy of the proposed method.

</details>


### [129] [Newton-Based Mixed Precision Iterative Refinement for Large-Scale Sparse Continuous-Time Algebraic Riccati Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14742)
*Juan Zhang,Yang Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于牛顿法的混合精度迭代精化框架，用于求解大规模稀疏连续时间代数Riccati方程（CARE），通过低精度求解初始近似和内部Lyapunov修正方程、高精度计算残差和更新解，在保证精度的同时提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模稀疏CARE问题的求解计算成本高昂，传统双精度实现在处理大规模问题时效率不足，亟需一种能在保持精度的同时加速计算的方法。

Method: 构建混合精度牛顿迭代精化框架：低精度计算初始近似和内部Lyapunov修正方程，高精度评估残差并更新解；引入因子分解与截断策略处理不定残差和牛顿修正项（保持半正定性、控制秩增长）；给出首阶舍入误差分析推导残差递推关系；基于ADI方法实现（NLR-ADI用于初始CARE近似，LR-ADI用于内部Lyapunov修正）。

Result: 误差分析揭示了混合精度精化稳定与Lyapunov算子条件阈值（由低精度内解的单位舍入决定）之间的关系。数值实验表明：在稠密CARE上验证了条件数效应；在大规模稀疏CARE上，混合精度框架比全双精度实现更快，且保持同等精度。

Conclusion: 混合精度迭代精化框架能有效加速大规模稀疏CARE求解，在保证精度的前提下显著提升计算效率，具有实用价值。

Abstract: We propose a Newton-based mixed precision iterative refinement framework for solving large-scale sparse continuous-time algebraic Riccati equations (CAREs). The framework computes the initial approximation and the inner Lyapunov correction equations in lower precision, while evaluating residuals and updating the solution in higher precision. To handle indefinite residuals and Newton correction terms in low-rank form, we introduce factor decomposition procedures with truncation strategies that preserve positive semidefiniteness and control rank growth. A first-order rounding error analysis derives a residual recurrence for the refinement process and relates stable mixed precision refinement to a Lyapunov operator conditioning threshold governed by the unit roundoff of the lower precision inner solves. We then present a concrete ADI-based realization, using NLR-ADI for the initial CARE approximation and LR-ADI for the inner Lyapunov correction equations. Compared with dense Lyapunov correction implementations, this realization reduces the main computations to shifted linear solves and low-rank factor operations, and we provide a solver-dependent complexity analysis. Numerical experiments on dense CARE over a range of condition numbers illustrate the conditioning effect described by the error analysis, and experiments on large-scale sparse CAREs show that the mixed precision framework is faster than the full double precision implementation while maintaining the same level of accuracy.

</details>


### [130] [On Hyperbolic Stochastic Galerkin Projections of Shallow Water Linearised Moment Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14750)
*Safiere Kuijpers,Julian Koellermeier*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种侵入式随机伽辽金公式，用于一维浅水线性化矩方程，采用广义多项式混沌展开的伪谱乘积。假设摩擦系数为不确定参数，推导了能量方程，分析了双曲性并引入正则化以确保线性情况下的双曲性。数值实验表明，与蒙特卡洛方法相比，该方法在相当精度下运行时间显著缩短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统浅水方程精度有限，浅水线性化矩方程虽可提高精度，但引入不确定参数后，需要有效的随机分析方法。现有非侵入式蒙特卡洛方法虽然准确但计算成本高，因此需要一种更高效的侵入式随机方法。

Method: 采用侵入式随机伽辽金公式，结合广义多项式混沌展开的伪谱乘积，对一维浅水线性化矩方程进行随机建模。推导了能量方程，分析了随机伽辽金投影后双曲性不保持的问题，并引入正则化以确保线性情况下双曲性。

Result: 数值测试表明，所提出的随机伽辽金公式与非侵入式蒙特卡洛方法相比，在达到相当精度的同时，运行时间显著缩短。

Conclusion: 侵入式随机伽辽金方法能够有效处理浅水线性化矩方程中的参数不确定性，在保证精度的前提下大幅提升计算效率。

Abstract: In this work, we present an intrusive stochastic Galerkin formulation of the one-dimensional shallow water linearised moment equations expressed in conservative variables, using the pseudospectral product for generalised polynomial chaos expansions. The shallow water linearised moment equations constitute a hyperbolic system of partial differential equations with an arbitrary number of equations that enhance the accuracy of the standard shallow water equations. Without loss of generality, we assume for both the theoretical analysis and the simulations that the uncertain parameter is the friction coefficient. For the new stochastic Galerkin shallow water linearised moment equations, we derive an energy equation, analyse the hyperbolicity - since this property is not preserved by the stochastic Galerkin projection - and introduce a regularisation to ensure hyperbolicity for the linear case of the shallow water linearised moment equations. Through numerical tests, we demonstrate the accuracy of the new stochastic Galerkin formulation in comparison with a non-intrusive Monte Carlo method, showing that the stochastic Galerkin approach achieves comparable accuracy with significantly faster run times.

</details>


### [131] [Structure-preserving Lagrange-multiplier methods for mean curvature flow and their error bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14787)
*Balázs Kovács,Buyang Li,Christian Lubich*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一类用于封闭曲面平均曲率流的演化曲面有限元方法，通过引入拉格朗日乘子保持能量递减结构，实现了时间步长上的面积递减特性，并证明了最优阶误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平均曲率流的数值模拟中，保持能量（面积）递减的物理结构是重要的，但现有方法难以严格保证这一性质。作者希望通过引入拉格朗日乘子来强制维持面积递减的离散结构。

Method: 基于Huisken演化方程（法向量和平均曲率），采用线性隐式BDF格式离散抛物型几何变量，结合隐式Adams方法更新节点位置，并引入拉格朗日乘子约束，同时允许最小变形率类型的切向速度。

Result: 证明离散算法在每个时间步都是面积递减的，递减率由计算的平均曲率决定。证明了离散拉格朗日乘子的局部存在唯一性及简化Newton迭代的收敛性，并导出了$H^1$范数下$h^k+\tau^q$阶的最优误差界（$k\ge2$，$2\le q\le5$）。

Conclusion: 拉格朗日乘子修正仅带来微小计算开销且与网格和步长大小基本无关，数值实验验证了收敛率和算法有效性。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a class of evolving surface finite element methods for mean curvature flow of closed surfaces using Lagrange multipliers for preserving the energy-decreasing structure. The algorithm is based on the solution-driven formulation using Huisken's evolution equations for the normal vector and the mean curvature. The time discretizations use linearly implicit backward difference formulas (BDF) for the parabolic geometric variables and implicit Adams updates for the nodal positions. The approach also accommodates artificial tangential velocities of minimal-deformation-rate type. The resulting fully discrete algorithms are area-decreasing at every time step, with a prescribed decay rate determined by the computed mean curvature. We prove local existence and uniqueness of the discrete Lagrange multiplier and convergence of a simplified Newton iteration for its computation under weak regularity assumptions. Under stronger regularity assumptions, as used in the convergence theory for the underlying evolving surface finite element method, we derive optimal-order error bounds of order $h^k+τ^q$ in the $H^1$-norm for finite elements of polynomial degree $k\ge 2$ and $q$-step BDF and $q$-step implicit Adams methods with $2\le q\le 5$, both without and with minimal-deformation-rate tangential motion. Numerical experiments for mean curvature flow of a sphere confirm the predicted convergence rates and show that the Lagrange-multiplier correction entails only a small computational overhead that is essentially independent of the mesh size and the time step size.

</details>


### [132] [An unfitted boundary algebraic equation method with static-dynamic reduction for evolving implicit geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14808)
*Yizhen Huang,Qing Xia*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于移动边界问题的静态-动态边界约化方法，基于无拟合晶格格林函数法，采用"先离散后表示"的策略，将边界交互分为静态和动态两部分，避免网格重建和奇异求积，支持平移、变形、出现及拓扑变化障碍物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动界面模拟、设计和反应导航中，频繁的椭圆求解因边界演化导致计算效率低下。即使使用固定笛卡尔网格避免网格重构，每次配置都重新计算所有边界交互仍会限制效率。

Method: 采用"离散化-再表示"的逆序思路：先离散笛卡尔算子，再构造格林表示；将边界交互分离为与静止几何相关的不变部分和与运动相关的变化部分；在真实界面交点处施加边界条件，复用晶格核数据，通过快速正弦变换重构内部场。

Result: 实现并验证了针对平移、变形、出现及拓扑变化障碍物的更新策略，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过静态-动态分离和离散化-再表示策略，显著提升了移动边界问题中重复椭圆求解的效率，避免了边界网格和奇异求积，适用于多种复杂边界演化场景。

Abstract: Repeated elliptic solves on domains with evolving boundaries arise in moving-interface simulation, design, and reactive navigation. Even when a fixed Cartesian grid avoids remeshing, rebuilding all boundary interactions for every configuration can limit the efficiency of repeated solves. We develop a static--dynamic boundary reduction for an unfitted lattice Green's function method on prescribed moving planar domains. Like boundary integral and boundary element methods, the formulation reduces the problem to boundary-supported unknowns through a Green representation. Its construction, however, reverses the usual order: the Cartesian operator is discretized before the Green representation is formed, rather than representing the continuous problem first and then discretizing the boundary. This discretize-then-represent viewpoint avoids boundary meshes and singular quadrature. The method also separates interactions associated with stationary geometry from those affected by motion, reuses the invariant part throughout a simulation, and updates only couplings involving the changing boundary. Boundary conditions are imposed at true interface intersections, lattice-kernel data are reused, and the interior field is reconstructed by a fast sine-transform solver. The principal contribution is an implemented and validated update strategy for translating, deforming, appearing, and topology-changing obstacles.

</details>


### [133] [Strong error analysis of a temporal approximation for stochastic Korteweg-de Vries equation with small additive noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14813)
*Jianbo Cui,Raffaele D'Ambrosio,Stefano Di Giovacchino,Liying Sun*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对小噪声驱动的随机KdV方程，通过将解分解为确定性KdV流和随机分量、线性化随机方程并结合傅里叶分析技术，首次证明了显式强收敛率：在H¹正则性下为𝒪(max(ε², τ, ετ^{1/2}))，在H²正则性下为𝒪(max(ε², τ))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机KdV方程的时间近似强误差分析因非线性项中的额外导数项以及精确解缺乏合适的指数矩界而极具挑战性，现有工作尚未给出显式的强收敛率。

Method: 利用小噪声机制，首先将解分解为确定性KdV流与随机分量；然后对所得随机方程进行线性化，并借助傅里叶分析技术逼近线性化方程；最后联合小噪声线性化误差、线性化方程的离散化误差以及确定性时间近似误差进行综合分析。

Result: 在H¹正则性下得到𝒪(max(ε², τ, ετ^{1/2}))的强收敛率，在H²正则性下得到𝒪(max(ε², τ))的强收敛率。

Conclusion: 首次为随机KdV方程的数值时间近似建立了显式强收敛率。

Abstract: We study strong temporal approximation of periodic stochastic Korteweg--de Vries equation driven by small additive \(Q\)-Wiener noise of amplitude \(\mathcal O(\varepsilon)\), \(0<\varepsilon\ll1\). Strong error analysis for temporal approximations of stochastic KdV is a challenging problem, due to the additional derivative term in the nonlinearity and thanks to the lack of suitable exponential moment bounds for the exact solutions. Exploiting the small-noise regime, we first decompose the solution into a deterministic KdV flow and a stochastic component; then we linearize the obtained stochastic equation and approximate the resulting equation by means of Fourier analytic techniques. Combining the small-noise linearization error, the discretization error of the linearized equation, and the deterministic temporal approximation error, we prove strong convergence rates of order \(\mathcal O(\max(\varepsilon^2,τ,\varepsilonτ^{1/2}))\) under \(H^1\)-regularity and \(\mathcal O(\max(\varepsilon^2,τ))\) under \(H^2\)-regularity, for the obtained approximation of the original stochastic KdV. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first explicit strong convergence rates shown for numerical time approximations of the stochastic KdV.

</details>


### [134] [Optimal complexity of adaptive FEM for second-order linear elliptic PDEs driven by non-residual estimators, Part I: Symmetric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14820)
*Philipp Bringmann,Aleksandar Dadic,Dario Ferloni,Gregor Gantner,Dirk Praetorius,Julian Streitberger*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对对称二阶线性椭圆型偏微分方程的自适应有限元方法，证明了在自适应算法同时驱动局部网格细化和迭代代数求解器的情况下，在适当的后验误差估计子和求解器的抽象假设下，算法无条件地达到完全R-线性收敛，且对用户选择的参数具有鲁棒性。对于足够小的参数，该方法能保证最优计算复杂度。与现有文献不同，本文重点分析了非残差型估计子（如基于平均的Zienkiewicz-Zhu估计子和基于平衡通量的估计子）的理论性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应有限元方法的收敛性和最优复杂性理论大多依赖于残差型后验误差估计子，而对于工程中广泛使用的非残差型估计子（如Zienkiewicz-Zhu平均估计子、平衡通量估计子）缺乏严格的理论分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立这类估计子在自适应算法中实现无条件收敛和最优复杂性的理论基础。

Method: 本文采用抽象公理化框架，对后验误差估计子和代数求解器提出一组抽象假设条件，在此框架下分析自适应有限元算法（同时包含网格细化和迭代求解器）的收敛性和计算复杂性。通过建立准误差（误差与估计子加权组合）的收缩性质，证明无条件全R-线性收敛，并利用非线性逼近类概念刻画最优复杂度。

Result: 证明了自适应算法在抽象假设下实现无条件全R-线性收敛，且收敛率对用户选择的参数具有独立性；对于足够小的参数，算法在非线性逼近类意义下达到最优计算复杂度；将理论结果成功应用于非残差型估计子，包括Zienkiewicz-Zhu型平均估计子和平衡通量估计子。

Conclusion: 本文建立的非残差型估计子的自适应有限元方法收敛性和最优复杂性理论，为工程实践中广泛使用的这类估计子提供了严格的数学基础，拓展了自适应有限元方法的理论适用范围。

Abstract: We consider adaptive finite element methods for symmetric second-order linear elliptic PDEs, where the adaptive algorithm steers the local mesh refinement as well as an iterative algebraic solver. Under abstract assumptions on the underlying a-posteriori error estimator and the solver, we prove that the usual adaptive algorithm leads to unconditional full R-linear convergence, independently of the user-chosen adaptivity parameters. For sufficiently small parameters, this guarantees optimal complexity in the sense that the decay rate of an appropriate quasi-error is optimal with respect to the overall computation cost (and hence time) measured in terms of the usual nonlinear approximation classes. Unlike available results in the literature, the main focus is on the analytical understanding of non-residual estimators like averaging-based estimators as proposed by Zienkiewicz and Zhu or estimators based on equilibrated fluxes.

</details>


### [135] [Subgrid-Scale Parameterization in Burgers' Equation Using Structure-Preserving Neural Networks and Entropy Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14855)
*Aijaz Nazir,Ilya Timofeyev*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于结构保持神经网络的亚网格参数化方法，将亚网格修正解耦为守恒通量势网络和涡黏性网络两部分，在粗粒度Burgers方程模拟中准确再现了全尺度系统的能量谱、时空相关函数和动力学特性，且对训练参数范围外的条件具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在粗粒度偏微分方程模拟中，亚网格尺度参数化是提升计算效率与物理保真度的关键挑战，传统方法难以同时保证结构保持性和泛化能力。

Method: 采用结构保持神经网络和熵变量学习亚网格通量，设计了一种解耦的神经网络架构，将亚网格校正显式分为两部分：保守通量势网络（Flux Potential）和涡黏性网络（Eddy Viscosity）。

Result: 该降阶框架在粗模拟中高保真地复现了全尺度系统的能量谱、空间和时间相关函数以及动力学特征，并且在训练参数范围外仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结构保持的解耦亚网格参数化方法能有效提升粗粒度模拟的物理保真度，且具有良好的参数外推能力，为复杂湍流系统的降阶建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present a machine learning approach for developing subgrid-scale (SGS) parametrizations in coarse simulations of partial differential equations. We utilize structure-preserving neural networks and entropy variables to learn subgrid fluxes in coarse simulations of the Burgers' equation. In particular, we employ a decoupled neural network architecture explicitly separating the subgrid corrections into two distinct components: a conservative Flux Potential network and an Eddy Viscosity network. We demonstrate that this reduced-order framework maintains high physical fidelity, accurately reproducing the energy spectrum, spatial and temporal correlation functions, and dynamical characteristics of the full-scale system. Furthermore, we show that our approach is robust and applicable to parameters outside the training regime.

</details>


### [136] [Fully discrete least-squares splitting scheme for the Monge-Ampère equation: finite element analysis and convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15024)
*Anna Peruso*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对二维Dirichlet Monge-Ampère方程的最小二乘分裂算法，提出了首个完整的有限元收敛性分析框架，在离散Miranda-Talenti估计和Hessian重构标准逼近性质下证明了迭代格式的局部收敛性及其极限在H²型范数下的最优阶收敛性，并验证了多种C¹和C⁰间断Galerkin格式的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小二乘分裂算法虽已成功用于Monge-Ampère方程计算多年，但全离散分裂格式的收敛理论长期缺失，亟需建立严格的理论基础。

Method: 提出有限元框架，结合离散Hessian重构与行列式约束的局部投影；基于离散Miranda-Talenti估计和Hessian重构的逼近性质，证明迭代格式的局部收敛性和H²范数下的最优阶收敛性。

Result: 验证了符合C¹的格式（如Argyris单元）及C⁰-内惩罚和DG格式（次数≥3）的收敛性；二次C⁰-内惩罚和DG格式在|u|_{H³(Ω)}充分小时也适用。数值实验确认理论收敛速率。

Conclusion: 首次为最小二乘分裂法提供了严格的有限元收敛理论，填补了该领域长期存在的理论空白。

Abstract: The least-squares splitting algorithm for the Monge-Ampère equation has been used successfully in computations for several years, but a convergence theory for fully discrete splitting schemes of this type has remained unavailable. In this work, we introduce and analyze a finite element framework for smooth solutions of the Dirichlet Monge-Ampère equation in two dimensions. The proposed schemes combine a discrete Hessian reconstruction with a local projection onto the determinant constraint. Under a discrete Miranda-Talenti estimate and standard approximation properties of the Hessian reconstruction, we prove local convergence of the iterative scheme and optimal-order convergence of its limit to the exact solution in an $H^2$-type norm. We verify the estimates for conforming $C^1$ schemes, including the Argyris element, and for $C^0$-interior penalty and DG schemes of degree at least three; quadratic $C^0$-interior penalty and DG schemes are also covered when $|u|_{H^3(Ω)}$ is sufficiently small. To the best of our knowledge, these discretizations have not previously been proposed or analyzed for least-squares splitting methods. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical convergence rates.

</details>


### [137] [An Adaptive and Physics-Preserving Multiscale Method for Two-Phase Flow Simulations in High-Contrast Heterogeneous Porous Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15063)
*Junhao Huang,Eric Chung,Wing Tat Leung*

Main category: math.NA

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an adaptive physics-preserving multiscale method for incompressible and immiscible two-phase flow in high-contrast porous media. The method couples a physics-preserving implicit-pressure explicit-saturation scheme (P-IMPES) with the mixed constraint energy minimizing generalized multiscale finite element method. The core algorithmic component is an adaptive update strategy for the saturation-dependent coefficient. Since the effective permeability \(κ_n=λ_t(S_w^n)K\) depends on the evolving saturation through the total mobility, we introduce an adaptive update algorithm that monitors the variation of the mobility-weighted coefficient and regenerates the multiscale spaces only when a prescribed tolerance is exceeded. A local postprocessing step is further used to recover fine-grid mass conservation. The analysis is a central part of the paper. We prove local conservation for both phases, the unbiased property of the phase formulation, and bounds preservation under a suitable CFL condition. For the advection-dominated case, we establish velocity and saturation error estimates, which clearly identify the contributions from the adaptive tolerance, the coarse mesh size, the spectral approximation, and the front-layer error. Numerical experiments on different high-contrast permeability fields confirm the physical properties of the method and show that smaller adaptive tolerances improve the saturation approximation while avoiding unnecessary updates of the multiscale spaces.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
